[31m+ export default function LiveVideoStudioPage() {[39m
[31m+   const { agents, loading: agentsLoading, degraded, refresh } = useAgents();[39m

[31m+   const businessAgents = useMemo([39m

[31m+     [agents],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   const [agentId, setAgentId] = useState("");[39m

[31m+   const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!businessAgents.length) return;[39m
[31m+     if (requestedAgentId && businessAgents.some((agent) => agent.id === requestedAgentId)) {[39m
[31m+       setAgentId(requestedAgentId);[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setAgentId((current) =>[39m
[31m+       current && businessAgents.some((agent) => agent.id === current)[39m
[31m+         ? current[39m
[31m+         : businessAgents[0].id,[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }, [businessAgents, requestedAgentId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const loadPresenter = useCallback(async (id: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const res = await authFetch(`/api/agents/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/video-presenter`);[39m
[31m+     const body = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as PresenterPayload & { error?: string };[39m
[31m+     if (!res.ok) throw new Error(body.error || "Could not load identity.");[39m
[31m+     return body;[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!agentId) return;[39m

[31m+     setLoadError(null);[39m
[31m+     setPayload(null);[39m
[31m+     void loadPresenter(agentId)[39m
[31m+       .then((body) => {[39m
[31m+         if (!cancelled) setPayload(body);[39m
[31m+       })[39m
[31m+       .catch((error: unknown) => {[39m
[31m+         if (cancelled) return;[39m
[31m+         setLoadError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load identity.");[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       cancelled = true;[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   }, [agentId, loadPresenter]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (!agentsLoading && degraded && !businessAgents.length) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <WorkspaceShell>[39m
[31m+         <WorkspaceRecovery onRetry={refresh} />[39m
[31m+       </WorkspaceShell>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m

[31m+         {loadError ? <StatusNotice tone="destructive">{loadError}</StatusNotice> : null}[39m
[31m+         {agentsLoading ? ([39m
[31m+           <StatusNotice>[39m
[31m+             <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" aria-hidden /> Loading agents[39m
[31m+           </StatusNotice>[39m
[31m+         ) : businessAgents.length ? ([39m
[31m+           <label className="block space-y-2">[39m
[31m+             <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Business agent</span>[39m
[31m+             <select[39m
[31m+               className="w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2"[39m
[31m+               value={agentId}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(event) => setAgentId(event.target.value)}[39m
[31m+               aria-label="Business agent"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {businessAgents.map((agent) => ([39m
[31m+                 <option key={agent.id} value={agent.id}>[39m
[31m+                   {agent.name}[39m
[31m+                 </option>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+             </select>[39m
[31m+           </label>[39m
[31m+         ) : ([39m
[31m+           <StatusNotice>[39m
[31m+             Create a business agent first, then come back for identity.{" "}[39m

[31m+               to="/studio?create=1&posture=business"[39m
[31m+               className="font-medium text-primary underline underline-offset-4"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               Create business agent[39m
[31m+             </Link>[39m
[31m+           </StatusNotice>[39m

[31m+             <AgentFace seed={payload.fallbackSeed} size={96} />[39m

[31m+   workspaceDisplayName: text("workspace_display_name"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceAccentHex: text("workspace_accent_hex"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceTheme: text("workspace_theme").default("obsidian").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   workspaceLogoUrl: text("workspace_logo_url"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceContactLine: text("workspace_contact_line"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceWirelineCreditVisible: boolean("workspace_wireline_credit_visible")[39m
[31m+     .default(true)[39m
[31m+     .notNull(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 1.0.1 SHAREABLE RECAP ARTIFACTS are defined in schema-base.ts.[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const spendBudgets = pgTable("wireline_spend_budgets", {[39m
[31m+   userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+     .primaryKey()[39m
[31m+     .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+   monthlyLimitUsd: decimal("monthly_limit_usd", {[39m
[31m+     precision: 12,[39m
[31m+     scale: 2,[39m
[31m+   })[39m
[31m+     .default("1.50")[39m
[31m+     .notNull(),[39m
[31m+   hardCap: boolean("hard_cap").default(true).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   warnThresholdPct: integer("warn_threshold_pct").default(80).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   enabled: boolean("enabled").default(true).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   managedDefault: boolean("managed_default").notNull().default(false),[39m
[31m+   updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Canonical billing mirror, including the provenance columns introduced by[39m
[31m+  * migration 0130. Keeping these fields on the primary runtime definition lets[39m
[31m+  * every entitlement writer state why non-Stripe access exists in the same[39m
[31m+  * atomic upsert that activates it.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const subscriptions = pgTable("wireline_subscriptions", {[39m
[31m+   userId: uuid("user_id").primaryKey(),[39m
[31m+   stripeCustomerId: text("stripe_customer_id"),[39m
[31m+   stripeSubscriptionId: text("stripe_subscription_id"),[39m
[31m+   stripePriceId: text("stripe_price_id"),[39m
[31m+   plan: text("plan"),[39m
[31m+   status: text("status"),[39m
[31m+   entitlementSource: text("entitlement_source", {[39m
[31m+     enum: ["stripe", "demo", "manual", "migration"],[39m
[31m+   })[39m
[31m+     .notNull()[39m
[31m+     .default("manual"),[39m
[31m+   grantReason: text("grant_reason"),[39m
[31m+   currentPeriodEnd: timestamp("current_period_end", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   cancelAtPeriodEnd: boolean("cancel_at_period_end").default(false),[39m
[31m+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 1.2.1 SPEND ALERTS are defined in schema-base.ts.[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any -- Drizzle's $type<...>()[39m
[31m+  * inference flows through generic .$type<T>() column callbacks where the[39m
[31m+  * shape is known by us but expressed as `any` in Drizzle's column-builder[39m
[31m+  * signatures (jsonb payloads, generated columns). Casting to the underlying[39m
[31m+  * runtime shape via `any` is the upstream-blessed escape hatch — see[39m
[31m+  * https://orm.drizzle.team for the typed-jsonb pattern. */[39m
[31m+ import {[39m
[31m+   pgTable,[39m
[31m+   text,[39m
[31m+   timestamp,[39m
[31m+   integer,[39m
[31m+   decimal,[39m
[31m+   boolean,[39m
[31m+   uuid,[39m
[31m+   bigint,[39m
[31m+   jsonb,[39m
[31m+   unique,[39m
[31m+   uniqueIndex,[39m
[31m+ } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";[39m
[31m+ import { relations, sql } from "drizzle-orm";[39m
[31m+ // Index support via sql`` for Drizzle ORM 0.33 compatibility[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // Schema version: latest committed migration is[39m
[31m+ // `drizzle/migrations/0154_xai_grok_46_persisted_defaults.sql`.[39m
[31m+ // Live Supabase schema may also be ahead via out-of-band applies — see AGENTS.md.[39m
[31m+ // After editing this file run `npm run db:generate` and review the new SQL before deploy.[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 0. IDENTITY & ACCOUNTS[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * A user can own one or two accounts — a personal account (single agent,[39m
[31m+  * streamlined UI) and/or a business account (AI employees, Studio command[39m
[31m+  * surfaces, outbound pipeline). The first account type created at signup becomes the[39m
[31m+  * "primary" account. Agents, settings, and billing scope to an account,[39m
[31m+  * not directly to a user.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const accounts = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_accounts",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+     type: text("type", { enum: ["personal", "business"] }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     label: text("label"),[39m
[31m+     isPrimary: boolean("is_primary").default(false),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userTypeIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_accounts_user_type ON wireline_accounts USING btree (user_id, type)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const users = pgTable("wireline_users", {[39m
[31m+   id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+   email: text("email").unique().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   passwordHash: text("password_hash"),[39m
[31m+   fullName: text("full_name"),[39m
[31m+   avatarUrl: text("avatar_url"),[39m
[31m+   isAdmin: boolean("is_admin").default(false),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Set when the user clicks the verification link we email them at[39m
[31m+    * signup. NULL means "unverified" — the trial gate refuses spend[39m
[31m+    * actions (outbound dial, new agent creation, Stripe checkout)[39m
[31m+    * until this is set. Existing users from before the verification[39m
[31m+    * gate shipped are backfilled to their createdAt (auto-verified)[39m
[31m+    * by the migration so they don't suddenly hit a paywall.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   emailVerifiedAt: timestamp("email_verified_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Single-use password-reset tokens with 1-hour TTL. The token itself is[39m
[31m+ // only stored hashed (sha256) so a DB leak doesn't enable account takeover.[39m
[31m+ export const passwordResetTokens = pgTable("wireline_password_reset_tokens", {[39m
[31m+   id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+   userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   tokenHash: text("token_hash").notNull().unique(),[39m
[31m+   expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   usedAt: timestamp("used_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   requestedIp: text("requested_ip"),[39m
[31m+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Single-use email-verification tokens with 24-hour TTL. Same hashed-[39m
[31m+  * storage pattern as password-reset tokens. Owned by a user; the[39m
[31m+  * email-service generates the plaintext token, stores only the sha256,[39m
[31m+  * and emails the plaintext to the user. They click the link, we look[39m
[31m+  * up by hash, verify expiry + usedAt, then set users.emailVerifiedAt[39m
[31m+  * and mark the token as used.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * Resend is rate-limited (1 token per minute per user) at the route[39m
[31m+  * layer to prevent abuse.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const emailVerificationTokens = pgTable("wireline_email_verification_tokens", {[39m
[31m+   id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+   userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   tokenHash: text("token_hash").notNull().unique(),[39m
[31m+   expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   usedAt: timestamp("used_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 1. SETTINGS & BILLING[39m
[31m+ export const settings = pgTable("wireline_settings", {[39m
[31m+   id: text("id").primaryKey(),[39m
[31m+   // FK with cascade so deleting a user removes their settings row in[39m
[31m+   // the same transaction — no orphans, no manual cleanup. The unique[39m
[31m+   // constraint stays: one settings row per user.[39m
[31m+   userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+     .unique()[39m
[31m+     .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+   subscriptionStatus: text("subscription_status").default("pending_activation"),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Wall-clock end of the 14-day soft trial that fires at signup.[39m
[31m+    * NULL on grandfathered accounts created before the trial gate[39m
[31m+    * shipped — those tenants stay on "active" forever.[39m
[31m+    *[39m
[31m+    * When subscriptionStatus = "trialing" and now() > trialEndsAt,[39m
[31m+    * the daily sweep flips status to "trial_expired" and routes that[39m
[31m+    * cost real money (outbound dial, new agent creation) return 402[39m
[31m+    * with an upgrade payload.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   trialEndsAt: timestamp("trial_ends_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Outbound-call counter scoped to the trial window. Increments on[39m
[31m+    * every successful POST /api/outbound/dial + /bulk during[39m
[31m+    * subscriptionStatus = "trialing". Capped at 25 by the trial-gate[39m
[31m+    * middleware. Reset to 0 when status flips to "active" via Stripe.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   trialOutboundCount: integer("trial_outbound_count").default(0),[39m
[31m+   maxAgents: integer("max_agents").default(3),[39m
[31m+   // Additional agents purchased à la carte beyond the plan's included[39m
[31m+   // max_agents. When this is N, the Account Holder can deploy[39m
[31m+   // (plan.max_agents + N) agents. Mutated only via the[39m
[31m+   // /api/billing/extra-agents endpoint which also adjusts the Stripe[39m
[31m+   // subscription item quantity for billing. Defaults to 0.[39m
[31m+   extraAgents: integer("extra_agents").default(0).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   /** Explicit ops/enterprise grant for photoreal live video streaming. */[39m
[31m+   liveVideoAddonEnabled: boolean("live_video_addon_enabled").default(false).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   vapiPrivateKey: text("vapi_private_key"),[39m
[31m+   vapiPhoneNumberId: text("vapi_phone_number_id"),[39m
[31m+   retentionDays: integer("retention_days").default(365),[39m
[31m+   updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   voiceLlmProvider: text("voice_llm_provider").default("xai"),[39m
[31m+   voiceLlmModel: text("voice_llm_model").default("grok-4.6"),[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefEnabled: boolean("daily_brief_enabled").default(false).notNull(),[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefSendAt: text("daily_brief_send_at").default("13:00").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefRecipient: text("daily_brief_recipient"),[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefLastSentAt: timestamp("daily_brief_last_sent_at", {[39m
[31m+     withTimezone: true,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefLastError: text("daily_brief_last_error"),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Short-lived durable lease for the cron dispatcher. This is never a[39m
[31m+    * delivery signal; dailyBriefLastSentAt is updated only after Gmail accepts[39m
[31m+    * the message. Stale leases are reclaimed by the scheduler.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefDispatchClaimedAt: timestamp("daily_brief_dispatch_claimed_at", {[39m
[31m+     withTimezone: true,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+   /** At-most-once latch for the current day's non-idempotent Gmail delivery. */[39m
[31m+   dailyBriefLastAttemptAt: timestamp("daily_brief_last_attempt_at", {[39m
[31m+     withTimezone: true,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+   businessName: text("business_name"),[39m
[31m+   /** Industry / vertical label for template overlays and Director context. */[39m
[31m+   businessType: text("business_type"),[39m
[31m+   /** Primary offer or product line — surfaced in Director Assistants planning. */[39m
[31m+   primaryProduct: text("primary_product"),[39m
[31m+   /** Tenant-wide operating hours (JSON) for template {{operatingHours}} substitution. */[39m
[31m+   businessHours: jsonb("business_hours"),[39m
[31m+   crmType: text("crm_type"),[39m
[31m+   twilioMessagingEnabled: boolean("twilio_messaging_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+   twilioLookupEnabled: boolean("twilio_lookup_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+   twilioManagerAlertsEnabled: boolean("twilio_manager_alerts_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+   twilioMessagingServiceSid: text("twilio_messaging_service_sid"),[39m
[31m+   twilioSmsFromNumber: text("twilio_sms_from_number"),[39m
[31m+   twilioWhatsappFromNumber: text("twilio_whatsapp_from_number"),[39m
[31m+   twilioManagerAlertNumber: text("twilio_manager_alert_number"),[39m
[31m+   vapiPhoneInboundAgentId: text("vapi_phone_inbound_agent_id"),[39m
[31m+   // Account Holder's personal callback number (E.164). When they "take" a live call[39m
[31m+   // from the Wireline app, the agent warm-transfers the caller here. iOS can't[39m
[31m+   // read the device's own number, so the user sets this once.[39m
[31m+   handoffPhone: text("handoff_phone"),[39m
[31m+   prospectingEnabled: boolean("prospecting_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+   targetTitles: jsonb("target_titles"),[39m
[31m+   targetIndustries: jsonb("target_industries"),[39m
[31m+   targetStates: jsonb("target_states"),[39m
[31m+   targetEmployeeRanges: jsonb("target_employee_ranges"),[39m
[31m+   brandName: text("brand_name"),[39m
[31m+   brandLogoUrl: text("brand_logo_url"),[39m
[31m+   primaryColor: text("primary_color"),[39m
[31m+   signupPlanInterest: text("signup_plan_interest"),[39m
[31m+   signupBillingInterval: text("signup_billing_interval"),[39m
[31m+   signupWedge: text("signup_wedge"),[39m
[31m+   acquisitionSource: text("acquisition_source"),[39m
[31m+   acquisitionMedium: text("acquisition_medium"),[39m
[31m+   acquisitionCampaign: text("acquisition_campaign"),[39m
[31m+   aiTier: text("ai_tier").default("starter"),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Tenant-wide default autonomy. Per-agent autonomy in `agents.autonomyLevel`[39m
[31m+    * overrides this when set to anything other than "inherit". Values:[39m
[31m+    * "auto" — execute actions immediately; "draft" — queue for human review;[39m
[31m+    * "supervised" — auto-execute high-confidence, draft the rest.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   autonomyLevel: text("autonomy_level").default("draft"),[39m
[31m+   /** Opt-in: send a 1-question CSAT SMS after completed calls. */[39m
[31m+   csatSmsEnabled: boolean("csat_sms_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * First useful production call — north-star activation timestamp.[39m
[31m+    * Set once by tenant-activation when a non-demo phone call clears quality bar.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   activatedAt: timestamp("activated_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+   firstActivationCallId: bigint("first_activation_call_id", { mode: "bigint" }),[39m
[31m+   timeToActivationSeconds: integer("time_to_activation_seconds"),[39m
[31m+   activationChannel: text("activation_channel"),[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * Workspace brand — carried into shareable recap and (future) proposal[39m
[31m+    * artifacts. Only affects the surface of published pages; the underlying[39m
[31m+    * Letterpress structure stays owned by Wireline. See[39m
[31m+    * `src/lib/shareable-artifact/brand.ts` and the migration[39m
[31m+    * `0102_shareable_recap_artifacts.sql`.[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   workspaceDisplayName: text("workspace_display_name"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceAccentHex: text("workspace_accent_hex"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceTheme: text("workspace_theme").default("obsidian").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   workspaceLogoUrl: text("workspace_logo_url"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceContactLine: text("workspace_contact_line"),[39m
[31m+   workspaceWirelineCreditVisible: boolean("workspace_wireline_credit_visible")[39m
[31m+     .default(true)[39m
[31m+     .notNull(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m

[31m+     .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m

[31m+ // 1.2.1 SPEND ALERTS[39m
[31m+ export const spendAlerts = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_spend_alerts",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     thresholdPct: integer("threshold_pct"),[39m
[31m+     period: text("period"),[39m
[31m+     firedAt: timestamp("fired_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_spend_alerts_user ON wireline_spend_alerts USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     // One alert row per tenant/period/threshold; evaluateSpend claims the row[39m
[31m+     // before dispatching so a busy month cannot re-fire the same webhook.[39m
[31m+     userPeriodThresholdUnique: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_spend_alerts_user_period_threshold ON wireline_spend_alerts (user_id, period, threshold_pct)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m

[31m+ // 1.3 DELETION QUEUE[39m
[31m+ export const deletionQueue = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_deletion_queue",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     requestedAt: timestamp("requested_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     scheduledPurgeAt: timestamp("scheduled_purge_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").default("pending"),[39m
[31m+     cancelledAt: timestamp("cancelled_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     purgedAt: timestamp("purged_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     ip: text("ip"),[39m
[31m+     userAgent: text("user_agent"),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_deletion_queue_user ON wireline_deletion_queue USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 2. Assistants INVENTORY (AGENTS)[39m
[31m+ export const agents = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_agents",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: text("id").primaryKey(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** Scopes the agent to a specific account. NULL for legacy agents[39m
[31m+      *  created before account separation — backfilled lazily on first[39m
[31m+      *  account-scoped request. */[39m
[31m+     accountId: uuid("account_id").references(() => accounts.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     voiceId: text("voice_id"),[39m
[31m+     voiceIdInbound: text("voice_id_inbound"),[39m
[31m+     voiceIdOutbound: text("voice_id_outbound"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Secondary voice for mid-call language swap. When set, the Vapi[39m
[31m+      * webhook listens for `language-change-detected` events and hands[39m
[31m+      * the live call off to a clone of this assistant with the secondary[39m
[31m+      * voiceId active — so the caller hears a native-sounding speaker[39m
[31m+      * the instant the transcriber flips from `en` to a non-English[39m
[31m+      * locale (typically Spanish for our customers).[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * Optional. Only meaningful when a primary voiceId is already set.[39m
[31m+      * NULL → no swap; caller continues with the primary voice.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * Implementation notes (Vapi API surface, May 2026 docs):[39m
[31m+      *   - Vapi PATCH /call/{id} does NOT mutate voice config on a live[39m
[31m+      *     call (only `name` is mutable). We instead use the call-control[39m
[31m+      *     `handoff` payload to swap voices, which adds ~500ms of dead[39m
[31m+      *     air during the handoff but is the only seamless path Vapi[39m
[31m+      *     supports today.[39m
[31m+      *   - Pattern source: Vapi call-control handoff for mid-call voice swaps.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     secondaryVoiceId: text("secondary_voice_id"),[39m
[31m+     voiceProvider: text("voice_provider").default("xai"),[39m
[31m+     /** PSTN line voice when studio voice uses presenter lip-sync. */[39m
[31m+     phoneVoiceId: text("phone_voice_id"),[39m
[31m+     phoneVoiceProvider: text("phone_voice_provider"),[39m
[31m+     firstMessage: text("first_message"),[39m
[31m+     archetype: text("archetype").default("Receptionist"),[39m
[31m+     ambientNoise: text("ambient_noise").default("off"),[39m
[31m+     websiteUrl: text("website_url"),[39m
[31m+     transferPhone: text("transfer_phone"),[39m
[31m+     emailAutoRespond: boolean("email_auto_respond").default(true),[39m
[31m+     emailAgentTone: text("email_agent_tone").default("Professional"),[39m
[31m+     emailSignature: text("email_signature"),[39m
[31m+     channel: text("channel").default("voice"),[39m
[31m+     role: text("role").default("customer_service"),[39m
[31m+     enabledTools: jsonb("enabled_tools"),[39m
[31m+     autonomyLevel: text("autonomy_level").default("inherit"),[39m
[31m+     channels: text("channels").array(),[39m
[31m+     slackChannelId: text("slack_channel_id"),[39m
[31m+     autoZoomLink: boolean("auto_zoom_link").default(false),[39m
[31m+     businessHours: jsonb("business_hours"),[39m
[31m+     timezone: text("timezone").default("UTC"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Personality vibe layered on top of the agent's role + archetype.[39m
[31m+      * Drives a single short directive block injected into the system[39m
[31m+      * prompt at runtime. Defaults to "warm" — the trustworthy + warm[39m
[31m+      * receptionist tone every vertical can wear. Account Holders bump to[39m
[31m+      * "playful" for retail / agencies / SaaS where memorability beats[39m
[31m+      * formality, or to "authoritative" for medical / legal / financial[39m
[31m+      * intake where the caller needs to feel they're in capable hands.[39m
[31m+      * Voice (Adam · Deep) stays consistent across vibes — vibe shifts[39m
[31m+      * the SCRIPT, not the voice.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     personalityVibe: text("personality_vibe").default("warm"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Account Holder-authored system prompt that fully REPLACES the[39m
[31m+      * auto-composed base identity prompt at call time. When set and[39m
[31m+      * non-empty, the Vapi assistant factory uses this text as the base[39m
[31m+      * identity block instead of the generated "## IDENTITY & MISSION"[39m
[31m+      * prompt (or the Wireline persona overlay), then still appends the[39m
[31m+      * call-specific dynamic blocks (caller profile, intel, sentiment,[39m
[31m+      * lead, flow, personality, directives, knowledge, lessons, date)[39m
[31m+      * AND runs the result through buildAgentRuntimeContext, so the[39m
[31m+      * compliance / escalation / do-not-discuss / voice-consistency[39m
[31m+      * guardrails can never be silently dropped.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * NULL (the default) → fall back to the auto-composed prompt,[39m
[31m+      * preserving existing behaviour for every agent that hasn't been[39m
[31m+      * overridden. Surfaced as the "System prompt" textarea in[39m
[31m+      * AssistantSettings → AgentConfigPanel with a "Use auto-composed prompt"[39m
[31m+      * reset that sets this back to NULL.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     systemPromptOverride: text("system_prompt_override"),[39m
[31m+     complianceMode: text("compliance_mode").default("standard"),[39m
[31m+     disallowedTopics: text("disallowed_topics").array(),[39m
[31m+     escalationKeywords: text("escalation_keywords").array(),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Per-agent escalation routing. When set, the `escalate_to_human`[39m
[31m+      * tool routes here INSTEAD of the tenant-wide[39m
[31m+      * `wireline_settings.twilio_manager_alert_number`. Lets account holders[39m
[31m+      * route sales escalations to the sales manager and support[39m
[31m+      * escalations to the support lead without splitting tenants.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * - escalationPhone : SMS destination (E.164 format)[39m
[31m+      * - escalationEmail : Email destination for non-instant escalations[39m
[31m+      *                     (delivered via Resend on the same job that[39m
[31m+      *                     posts to Slack + SMS)[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * If BOTH are NULL the handler falls back to the tenant defaults,[39m
[31m+      * preserving existing behaviour for accounts that haven't migrated.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     escalationPhone: text("escalation_phone"),[39m
[31m+     escalationEmail: text("escalation_email"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Warm-transfer toggle. When TRUE and escalation_phone is set, the[39m
[31m+      * `escalate_to_human` tool returns a Vapi `destination` so the LIVE[39m
[31m+      * caller is transferred to escalation_phone in real time instead of[39m
[31m+      * just queueing an async SMS/email alert. The product-defining[39m
[31m+      * "AI receptionist that warm-transfers to a human" promise.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     escalationHandoffEnabled: boolean("escalation_handoff_enabled").default(false),[39m
[31m+     businessVariables: jsonb("business_variables"),[39m
[31m+     flowConfig: jsonb("flow_config"),[39m
[31m+     outOfHoursMessage: text("out_of_hours_message"),[39m
[31m+     kpiFocus: text("kpi_focus"),[39m
[31m+     isBusinessAgent: boolean("is_business_agent").default(false).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     // Per-archetype silence tolerance for Vapi calls. NULL → factory uses[39m
[31m+     // archetype default (Receptionist 5s, SDR 2s, etc.).[39m
[31m+     silenceTimeoutSeconds: integer("silence_timeout_seconds"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Per-agent speaking rate multiplier. Range 0.7 – 1.2,[39m
[31m+      * default 1.0 (normal pace). Account Holders slow agents down for elderly[39m
[31m+      * customers (0.85) or speed them up for impatient callers (1.1).[39m
[31m+      * Surfaced as a slider in AssistantSettings → Voice and wired into the[39m
[31m+      * Vapi assistant voice block as `voice.speed`.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * Stored as numeric (Postgres `numeric`) for exact arithmetic; the[39m
[31m+      * voice factory coerces via Number() before passing to Vapi.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     voiceSpeed: decimal("voice_speed", { precision: 3, scale: 2 }).default("1.0"),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Per-agent reasoning-model override (customization-first Agent Studio).[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * NULL on any of these → the agent inherits the tenant default[39m
[31m+      * (settings.voice_llm_provider / voice_llm_model) and the archetype's[39m
[31m+      * temperature, exactly as before. When set, the voice factory routes the[39m
[31m+      * live call through this provider/model instead. Values are validated +[39m
[31m+      * normalized by resolveVapiVoiceModelSelection() against the Vapi-native[39m
[31m+      * provider set (anthropic | google | openai | xai), so a bad[39m
[31m+      * value can never reach Vapi — it falls back to the tenant default.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * llm_temperature is clamped to a safe range in the factory so a per-agent[39m
[31m+      * override can't destabilize the live tier. See VOICE_MODEL_CATALOG.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     llmProvider: text("llm_provider"),[39m
[31m+     llmModel: text("llm_model"),[39m
[31m+     llmTemperature: decimal("llm_temperature", { precision: 3, scale: 2 }),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Legacy voice fine-tuning knobs account holders used to dial in a custom[39m
[31m+      * sound. Retained as nullable columns for continuity with historical rows.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      *   stability         (0..1, default 0.5)  — voice consistency. Low =[39m
[31m+      *                      expressive/emotional, high = monotone/consistent.[39m
[31m+      *   similarity_boost  (0..1, default 0.75) — how closely TTS hugs the[39m
[31m+      *                      source voice.[39m
[31m+      *   style             (0..1, default 0.0)  — style exaggeration. >0[39m
[31m+      *                      adds latency but more character.[39m
[31m+      *   speaker_boost     (bool, default true) — boosts perceived loudness/[39m
[31m+      *                      presence; off for "intimate" voices.[39m
[31m+      *[39m
[31m+      * Numerics stored as `numeric(3,2)` (returned as a string by pg) — the[39m
[31m+      * factory coerces + clamps to [0,1] before send.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     voiceStability: decimal("voice_stability", {[39m
[31m+       precision: 3,[39m
[31m+       scale: 2,[39m
[31m+     }).default("0.5"),[39m
[31m+     voiceSimilarityBoost: decimal("voice_similarity_boost", {[39m
[31m+       precision: 3,[39m
[31m+       scale: 2,[39m
[31m+     }).default("0.75"),[39m
[31m+     voiceStyle: decimal("voice_style", { precision: 3, scale: 2 }).default("0.0"),[39m
[31m+     voiceSpeakerBoost: boolean("voice_speaker_boost").default(true),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Live video presenter (LiveAvatar LITE). Provider stays internal; UI reads[39m
[31m+      * Wireline surface labels from lib/agent-identity. avatar_id is the[39m
[31m+      * LiveAvatar catalog UUID. consent_status gates custom avatars.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     videoPresenterProvider: text("video_presenter_provider"),[39m
[31m+     videoPresenterAvatarId: text("video_presenter_avatar_id"),[39m
[31m+     videoPresenterConsentStatus: text("video_presenter_consent_status").default("not_started"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX idx_agents_user_id ON wireline_agents USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     createdAtIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX idx_agents_created_at ON wireline_agents USING btree (created_at)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Tenant-owned instant voices created through the founder-managed ElevenLabs[39m
[31m+  * account. Uploaded source audio is never stored; only a one-way fingerprint,[39m
[31m+  * consent evidence, review state, and provider voice id remain. A voice cannot[39m
[31m+  * enter the phone runtime until status is `approved`.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const clonedVoices = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_cloned_voices",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     voiceId: text("voice_id").primaryKey(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     provider: text("provider").notNull().default("elevenlabs"),[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").notNull().default("creating"),[39m
[31m+     previewUrl: text("preview_url"),[39m
[31m+     consentVersion: text("consent_version").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     sourceSha256: text("source_sha256").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     sourceMimeType: text("source_mime_type").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     sourceDurationSeconds: integer("source_duration_seconds").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     intendedUse: text("intended_use").notNull().default("wireline-agents"),[39m
[31m+     sourceAudioRetained: boolean("source_audio_retained").notNull().default(false),[39m
[31m+     isolationApplied: boolean("isolation_applied").notNull().default(false),[39m
[31m+     providerVerificationRequired: boolean("provider_verification_required")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .default(false),[39m
[31m+     qualityCheckVersion: text("quality_check_version"),[39m
[31m+     qualitySampleModel: text("quality_sample_model"),[39m
[31m+     approvedAt: timestamp("approved_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     approvedBy: uuid("approved_by").references(() => users.id, {[39m
[31m+       // Approval is durable authorization evidence. Do not let deleting a[39m
[31m+       // reviewer silently erase it while an approved clone remains live.[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "restrict",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     rejectionReason: text("rejection_reason"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userStatusCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cloned_voices_user_status_created ON wireline_cloned_voices USING btree (user_id, status, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     sourceFingerprintIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cloned_voices_source_fingerprint ON wireline_cloned_voices USING btree (user_id, source_sha256)`,[39m
[31m+     approvedByIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cloned_voices_approved_by ON wireline_cloned_voices USING btree (approved_by)`,[39m
[31m+     activeSourceUniqueIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_cloned_voices_active_source ON wireline_cloned_voices (user_id, source_sha256) WHERE status NOT IN ('revoked', 'failed')`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 2.1 COGNITIVE ENGINE (TRAINING DATA)[39m
[31m+ export const directives = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_directives",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     content: text("content").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     priority: integer("priority").default(5),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     agentUserPriorityIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_directives_agent_user_priority ON wireline_directives USING btree (agent_id, user_id, priority DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_directives_user_id ON wireline_directives USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const knowledge = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_knowledge",[39m

[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     content: text("content").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     agentUserIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_knowledge_agent_user ON wireline_knowledge USING btree (agent_id, user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_knowledge_user_created ON wireline_knowledge USING btree (user_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const personality = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_personality",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id")[39m
[31m+       .primaryKey()[39m
[31m+       .references(() => agents.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     content: text("content").notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_personality_user_id ON wireline_personality USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const agentLessons = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_agent_lessons",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     lesson: text("lesson").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     sourceCallId: text("source_call_id"),[39m
[31m+     source: text("source"),[39m
[31m+     /** pending | approved | rejected — only approved lessons inject into runtime. */[39m
[31m+     lessonStatus: text("lesson_status").default("approved"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   (t: any) => ({[39m
[31m+     unq: unique().on(t.agentId, t.lesson),[39m
[31m+     agentCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_lessons_agent_created ON wireline_agent_lessons USING btree (agent_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Per-agent Account Holder chat thread. Persistent, ongoing — distinct from[39m
[31m+ // /train which is structural agent configuration. Think of this as the[39m
[31m+ // private DM channel the Account Holder has with each AI agent: ongoing[39m
[31m+ // direction, coaching, "how did the calls go this week?", file-attached[39m
[31m+ // context. Memory is the thread itself plus the agent's flow + recent[39m
[31m+ // call summaries injected as context at LLM time.[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // One thread per (userId, agentId). When the Account Holder sends, we store[39m
[31m+ // a row with role="Account Holder". Claude reads thread history + agent[39m
[31m+ // config + recent calls, replies, and we store the reply with[39m
[31m+ // role="agent". Soft-delete on agent delete via cascade.[39m
[31m+ export const agentConversationMessages = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_agent_conversation_messages",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id")[39m
[31m+       .references(() => agents.id, { onDelete: "cascade" })[39m
[31m+       .notNull(),[39m
[31m+     // role is one of "Account Holder" | "agent" — DB-level CHECK constraint[39m
[31m+     // lives in the migration (this Drizzle version doesn't expose .$type()).[39m
[31m+     role: text("role").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     content: text("content").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** Claude's adaptive thinking/reasoning text for this message (nullable). */[39m
[31m+     reasoning: text("reasoning"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userAgentIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_conv_user_agent ON wireline_agent_conversation_messages USING btree (user_id, agent_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Director chat — the account-level strategist thread.[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // Unlike agentConversationMessages (one thread per agent), the Director is a[39m
[31m+ // single per-USER thread. It's always available — even before the Account Holder[39m
[31m+ // owns any agents — and acts as the manager that helps plan, set up, and[39m
[31m+ // coach the Assistants. role is "Account Holder" | "director". No agent FK: the Director[39m
[31m+ // exists at the account level, so its history survives agent deletions.[39m
[31m+ export const directorConversationMessages = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_director_conversation_messages",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** Conversation thread key. "director" = the Assistants chat. */[39m
[31m+     threadKey: text("thread_key").notNull().default("director"),[39m
[31m+     // role is one of "Account Holder" | "director" — DB-level CHECK constraint[39m
[31m+     // lives in the migration (this Drizzle version doesn't expose .$type()).[39m
[31m+     role: text("role").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     content: text("content").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** Claude's adaptive thinking/reasoning text for this message (nullable). */[39m
[31m+     reasoning: text("reasoning"),[39m
[31m+     /** Structured reply payload — proposals, suggestions, creative approvals, etc. */[39m
[31m+     metadata: jsonb("metadata"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_director_conv_user ON wireline_director_conversation_messages USING btree (user_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     userThreadIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_director_conv_user_thread_created ON wireline_director_conversation_messages USING btree (user_id, thread_key, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Director artifacts — persisted generated files and their backing objects.[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // Storage model:[39m
[31m+ // 1) wireline_director_artifact_objects stores the raw base64 payload keyed by[39m
[31m+ //    object_key (our object-store abstraction).[39m
[31m+ // 2) wireline_director_artifacts stores per-thread/version metadata and points[39m
[31m+ //    at the object key for signed delivery.[39m
[31m+ export const directorArtifactObjects = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_director_artifact_objects",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     objectKey: text("object_key").primaryKey(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     mimeType: text("mime_type").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     base64Data: text("base64_data").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     sizeBytes: integer("size_bytes").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_director_artifact_objects_user_created ON wireline_director_artifact_objects USING btree (user_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const directorArtifacts = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_director_artifacts",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     threadKey: text("thread_key").notNull().default("director"),[39m
[31m+     messageId: bigint("message_id", { mode: "bigint" }),[39m
[31m+     toolName: text("tool_name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     artifactType: text("artifact_type").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     objectKey: text("object_key").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     metadata: jsonb("metadata").default({}),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userThreadIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_director_artifacts_user_thread_created ON wireline_director_artifacts USING btree (user_id, thread_key, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     objectIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_director_artifacts_object ON wireline_director_artifacts USING btree (object_key)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m

[31m+ // 3. SIGNAL ARCHIVES (CALL LOGS)[39m
[31m+ export const callLogs = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_call_logs",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     vapiCallId: text("vapi_call_id").unique(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id),[39m
[31m+     direction: text("direction"),[39m
[31m+     customerNumber: text("customer_number"),[39m
[31m+     durationSeconds: integer("duration_seconds"),[39m
[31m+     cost: decimal("cost", { precision: 10, scale: 4 }),[39m
[31m+     summary: text("summary"),[39m
[31m+     transcript: text("transcript"),[39m
[31m+     recordingUrl: text("recording_url"),[39m
[31m+     leadScore: integer("lead_score").default(0),[39m
[31m+     intentLabel: text("intent_label").default("none"),[39m
[31m+     sentiment: text("sentiment").default("neutral"),[39m
[31m+     coachingNote: text("coaching_note"),[39m
[31m+     qaScore: integer("qa_score"),[39m
[31m+     qaBreakdown: jsonb("qa_breakdown"),[39m
[31m+     /** Post-call CSAT score (1-5) from the caller's SMS reply. */[39m
[31m+     csatScore: integer("csat_score"),[39m
[31m+     // Single-word outcome bucket the post-call digest uses for the email[39m
[31m+     // subject + Insights breakdown: booked | qualified | followup | dnc |[39m
[31m+     // unreachable | info | other.[39m
[31m+     outcomeTag: text("outcome_tag"),[39m
[31m+     // Campaign attribution — denormalized from phoneNumbers.campaignLabel at[39m
[31m+     // write time so /insights queries don't need a join.[39m
[31m+     campaignSource: text("campaign_source"),[39m
[31m+     // True when the row was inserted by /api/admin/seed-demo-account so[39m
[31m+     // the UI can render a "DEMO" pill and account holders won't mistake seeded[39m
[31m+     // showcase activity for real call traffic.[39m
[31m+     demo: boolean("demo").default(false).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // Voice observability — per-call telemetry for TTFT, STT/TTS health, pipeline latency.[39m
[31m+     // Written by voice webhooks + recordVoiceMetrics(); queried by GET /api/insights/latency[39m
[31m+     // and GET /api/health/voice-status (see src/lib/voice-observability.ts).[39m
[31m+     ttftMs: integer("ttft_ms"), // Time-to-first-token (target: <900ms, alert: >1.2s)[39m
[31m+     ttfAudioMs: integer("ttf_audio_ms"), // Time-to-first-audio (full pipeline)[39m
[31m+     sttError: boolean("stt_error").default(false), // Speech-to-text failed/timeout[39m
[31m+     ttsError: boolean("tts_error").default(false), // Text-to-speech failed/interrupted[39m
[31m+     pipelineError: text("pipeline_error"), // High-level error classification: stt|tts|llm|connection|unknown[39m
[31m+     latencyP50Ms: integer("latency_p50_ms"), // Provider-reported p50 latency (if available)[39m
[31m+     latencyP95Ms: integer("latency_p95_ms"), // Provider-reported p95 latency (if available)[39m
[31m+     /** Vapi call end reason — distinct from pipeline_error (see voice-reliability dashboard). */[39m
[31m+     endedReason: text("ended_reason"),[39m
[31m+     toolCallsTotal: integer("tool_calls_total").default(0),[39m
[31m+     toolCallsFailed: integer("tool_calls_failed").default(0),[39m
[31m+     escalationAttempted: boolean("escalation_attempted").default(false),[39m
[31m+     escalationSucceeded: boolean("escalation_succeeded").default(false),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_user_id ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_user_created ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (user_id, created_at)`,[39m
[31m+     agentIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_agent_id ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (agent_id)`,[39m
[31m+     agentCustomerCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_agent_customer_created ON wireline_call_logs (agent_id, customer_number, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     createdAtIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_created_at ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (created_at)`,[39m
[31m+     userOutcomeIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_user_outcome ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (user_id, outcome_tag) WHERE outcome_tag IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     userCampaignIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_user_campaign ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (user_id, campaign_source) WHERE campaign_source IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     // Voice observability indexes — used by computeVoiceHealthWindow() + latency trends[39m
[31m+     ttftIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_ttft ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (ttft_ms) WHERE ttft_ms IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     demoCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_demo_created ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (demo, created_at) WHERE demo = true`,[39m
[31m+     pipelineErrorIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_pipeline_error ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (pipeline_error) WHERE pipeline_error IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     endedReasonIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_ended_reason ON wireline_call_logs USING btree (ended_reason) WHERE ended_reason IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     userPhoneTailIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_logs_user_phone_tail ON wireline_call_logs (user_id, right(regexp_replace(customer_number, '[^0-9]', '', 'g'), 10))`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.1b SMS THREADS + MESSAGES — two-way text conversations.[39m
[31m+ // Mirrors the email_threads/email_messages shape so the SmsThreads UI[39m
[31m+ // can reuse the same inbox layout. One thread per (user, customer phone)[39m
[31m+ // pair; messages append in time order. Inbound writes from the Twilio[39m
[31m+ // webhook; outbound writes from twilio-service.sendTenantMessage.[39m
[31m+ export const smsThreads = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_sms_threads",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "set null",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     customerNumber: text("customer_number").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").default("open"),[39m
[31m+     summary: text("summary"),[39m
[31m+     unreadCount: integer("unread_count").default(0).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     lastMessageAt: timestamp("last_message_at", {[39m
[31m+       withTimezone: true,[39m
[31m+     }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userCustomerUnique: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sms_threads_user_customer ON wireline_sms_threads (user_id, customer_number)`,[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sms_threads_user_id ON wireline_sms_threads (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     lastMessageIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sms_threads_last_message ON wireline_sms_threads (last_message_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const smsMessages = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_sms_messages",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     threadId: uuid("thread_id").references(() => smsThreads.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     direction: text("direction").notNull(), // 'in' or 'out'[39m
[31m+     body: text("body").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     twilioSid: text("twilio_sid"),[39m
[31m+     /** Optional agent that authored an outbound message. Null for inbound + Account Holder replies. */[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m

[31m+     twilioSidUniqueIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sms_messages_user_twilio_sid ON wireline_sms_messages (user_id, twilio_sid) WHERE twilio_sid IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.1c AGENT TEAMS + CALL QUEUES — call-center scaffolding.[39m
[31m+ // Distinct from the human-team RBAC concept in wireline_team_members[39m
[31m+ // (workspace user invites). These tables group AI AGENTS into teams[39m
[31m+ // for call-center routing — one tenant has many agent teams (e.g.[39m
[31m+ // "Inbound Support", "Outbound Sales"). Each agent team optionally[39m
[31m+ // overrides the platform Twilio sender. Call queues sit one level[39m
[31m+ // deeper: when a customer dials a queue's number, we route to the[39m
[31m+ // least-busy agent in that queue's team.[39m
[31m+ export const agentTeams = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_agent_teams",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** Per-team SMS sender — overrides settings.twilio_sms_from_number for agents in this team. */[39m
[31m+     smsFromNumber: text("sms_from_number"),[39m
[31m+     /** Optional manager email — destination for escalations from this team. */[39m
[31m+     managerEmail: text("manager_email"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_teams_user_id ON wireline_agent_teams (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const agentTeamMembers = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_agent_team_members",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     // Tenant scoping. agent_team_members was historically scoped only via the[39m
[31m+     // team_id FK, but as soon as a route queries it directly (without joining[39m
[31m+     // through wireline_agent_teams) RLS has nothing to enforce. Carry the[39m
[31m+     // owning user_id on the row so the standard[39m
[31m+     //   user_id = NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_user_id', true), '')::uuid[39m
[31m+     // policy applies (see migration 0026).[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     teamId: uuid("team_id").references(() => agentTeams.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     /** "member" or "lead" — leads get first routing preference. */[39m
[31m+     role: text("role").default("member"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     teamAgentUnique: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_team_members_team_agent ON wireline_agent_team_members (team_id, agent_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_team_members_user_id ON wireline_agent_team_members (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export const callQueues = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_call_queues",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     teamId: uuid("team_id").references(() => agentTeams.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "cascade",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     /** vapi_number_id this queue answers on. */[39m
[31m+     vapiNumberId: text("vapi_number_id"),[39m
[31m+     /** Max seconds before overflow_action triggers. */[39m
[31m+     maxWaitSeconds: integer("max_wait_seconds").default(60),[39m
[31m+     /** "voicemail" | "transfer" | "queue" — what happens when no agent is free. */[39m
[31m+     overflowAction: text("overflow_action").default("voicemail"),[39m
[31m+     /** Phone number to transfer to when overflow_action='transfer'. */[39m
[31m+     overflowTransferTo: text("overflow_transfer_to"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_queues_user_id ON wireline_call_queues (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     vapiNumberIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_call_queues_vapi_number ON wireline_call_queues (vapi_number_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Multi-touch outbound cadences. A cadence is a sequence of steps (call /[39m
[31m+  * sms / email) with delays between them. Account Holders define one once; the[39m
[31m+  * sentinel job worker ticks through it for every lead enrolled.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * Example steps payload:[39m
[31m+  *   [[39m
[31m+  *     { type: "call",  delayHours: 0  },[39m
[31m+  *     { type: "sms",   delayHours: 24, template: "Hi {{name}}, following up on our call..." },[39m
[31m+  *     { type: "email", delayHours: 72, template: "..." },[39m
[31m+  *     { type: "call",  delayHours: 168 }[39m
[31m+  *   ][39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const cadences = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_cadences",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     description: text("description"),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id"),[39m
[31m+     steps: jsonb("steps").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     active: boolean("active").default(true),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadences_user_id ON wireline_cadences (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Per-lead cadence execution state. One row per (lead, cadence) enrollment.[39m
[31m+  * Sentinel scans this table for rows where next_due_at <= now() AND status =[39m
[31m+  * 'active', executes the step, advances the counter, schedules next.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const cadenceRuns = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_cadence_runs",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     cadenceId: uuid("cadence_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     leadId: bigint("lead_id", { mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     currentStep: integer("current_step").default(0).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     nextDueAt: timestamp("next_due_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").default("active"),[39m
[31m+     lastResult: text("last_result"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     dueIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_runs_due ON wireline_cadence_runs (status, next_due_at) WHERE status = 'active'`,[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_runs_user_id ON wireline_cadence_runs (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     // Scoped to active runs: a full unique here means a lead whose run[39m
[31m+     // completed can never be re-enrolled in the same cadence (409 forever).[39m
[31m+     leadIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadence_runs_lead_unique ON wireline_cadence_runs (lead_id, cadence_id) WHERE status = 'active'`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Live call supervisor subscriptions — supervisor clicks "Listen in" or[39m
[31m+  * "Whisper" on a live call in Studio / Phone, we capture the intent here. V1 is[39m
[31m+  * a bookmark + later audit; V2 wires Vapi's WebRTC streaming for real-time[39m
[31m+  * audio.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const supervisorSubscriptions = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_supervisor_subscriptions",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     callId: text("call_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     mode: text("mode").default("listen"),[39m
[31m+     note: text("note"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     callIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supervisor_subs_call ON wireline_supervisor_subscriptions (call_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supervisor_subs_user ON wireline_supervisor_subscriptions (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.2 Assistants HANDOFF (TASKS)[39m
[31m+ export const followUpTasks = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_follow_up_tasks",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m

[31m+      * Action payload for "draft" autonomy items — the proposed email body,[39m

[31m+      * `action.payload.<field>`. Distinct from `customerInfo`, which is[39m

[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fleet_tasks_user_id ON wireline_follow_up_tasks USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     statusIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fleet_tasks_status ON wireline_follow_up_tasks USING btree (status)`,[39m
[31m+     userStatusIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fleet_tasks_user_status ON wireline_follow_up_tasks USING btree (user_id, status)`,[39m
[31m+     createdAtIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_fleet_tasks_created_at ON wireline_follow_up_tasks USING btree (created_at)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.3 NETWORK LINKS (PHONE NUMBERS)[39m
[31m+ export const phoneNumbers = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_phone_numbers",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m

[31m+     vapiNumberId: text("vapi_number_id"),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id),[39m
[31m+     // Marketing-campaign label so /insights can break down "calls from X ad".[39m
[31m+     // Free-form ("Google Ads — Spring '26", "LinkedIn — Founder Pitch", etc.).[39m
[31m+     campaignLabel: text("campaign_label"),[39m

[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m

[31m+     agentIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_phone_numbers_agent_id ON wireline_phone_numbers USING btree (agent_id)`,[39m
[31m+     // UNIQUE: inbound webhooks resolve the owning tenant by vapi_number_id with[39m
[31m+     // LIMIT 1, so a second tenant holding a row for the same Vapi line would[39m
[31m+     // silently capture the call. Migration 0111.[39m
[31m+     vapiNumberIdIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_phone_numbers_vapi_number_id_unique ON wireline_phone_numbers (vapi_number_id) WHERE vapi_number_id IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.4 OUTBOUND SIGNAL (LEADS & CAMPAIGNS)[39m
[31m+ export const outboundCampaigns = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_outbound_campaigns",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     name: text("name").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     goal: text("goal"),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "set null",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").default("draft"),[39m
[31m+     callWindowStart: text("call_window_start").default("09:00"),[39m
[31m+     callWindowEnd: text("call_window_end").default("17:00"),[39m
[31m+     maxAttempts: integer("max_attempts").default(3),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_campaigns_user_id ON wireline_outbound_campaigns USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_campaigns_user_created ON wireline_outbound_campaigns USING btree (user_id, created_at)`,[39m
[31m+     statusIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_campaigns_status ON wireline_outbound_campaigns USING btree (status)`,[39m
[31m+     agentIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_campaigns_agent_id ON wireline_outbound_campaigns USING btree (agent_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Outbound Leads - Lead state machine:[39m
[31m+  *   Pending -> Queued -> Dialing -> Connected -> (Completed | Voicemail-Left[39m
[31m+  *     | Callback-Scheduled | Failed)[39m
[31m+  *   Terminal states: Completed, Failed, Blocked-DNC, Max-Attempts[39m
[31m+  *   Sticky retry states (preserved by tools): Voicemail-Left, Callback-Scheduled[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export const outboundLeads = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_outbound_leads",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     agentId: text("agent_id").references(() => agents.id),[39m
[31m+     campaignId: uuid("campaign_id").references(() => outboundCampaigns.id, {[39m
[31m+       onDelete: "set null",[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     phoneNumber: text("phone_number"),[39m
[31m+     customerNumber: text("customer_number"),[39m
[31m+     customerName: text("customer_name"),[39m
[31m+     email: text("email"),[39m
[31m+     context: text("context"),[39m
[31m+     // Status values: Pending, Queued, Dialing, Connected, Completed,[39m
[31m+     // Voicemail-Left, Callback-Scheduled, Failed, Blocked-DNC, Max-Attempts[39m
[31m+     status: text("status").default("Pending"),[39m
[31m+     callId: text("call_id"),[39m
[31m+     lastCalledAt: timestamp("last_called_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * When the agent or Account Holder scheduled the next outreach attempt. Drives[39m
[31m+      * `/api/callbacks` and the "Upcoming Callbacks" pane in OutboundDialer.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     callbackScheduledAt: timestamp("callback_scheduled_at", {[39m
[31m+       withTimezone: true,[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * Short free-text summary of the most recent contact attempt. Surfaced[39m
[31m+      * in the callbacks UI and written by the AI Action Engine `update_crm`[39m
[31m+      * handler.[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     lastContactSummary: text("last_contact_summary"),[39m
[31m+     attempts: integer("attempts").default(0),[39m
[31m+     source: text("source").default("manual"),[39m
[31m+     // Pipeline kanban stage. Independent of `status` (which tracks dial state)[39m
[31m+     // — this tracks deal progression: cold → contacted → qualified → demo →[39m
[31m+     // won | lost. UI groups leads by this column on /pipeline.[39m
[31m+     pipelineStage: text("pipeline_stage").default("cold"),[39m
[31m+     // Marketing campaign attribution — set by csv-upload or auto-derived from[39m
[31m+     // the inbound number's campaignLabel.[39m
[31m+     campaignSource: text("campaign_source"),[39m
[31m+     metadata: jsonb("metadata"),[39m
[31m+     receivedAt: timestamp("received_at", { withTimezone: true }),[39m
[31m+     idempotencyKey: text("idempotency_key"),[39m
[31m+     // True when the lead was inserted by /api/admin/seed-demo-account. UI[39m
[31m+     // surfaces a "DEMO" pill on these rows so account holders don't mistake the[39m
[31m+     // showcase pipeline for real customer leads. /api/admin/demo-data[39m
[31m+     // (DELETE) wipes every row where demo=true scoped to the tenant.[39m
[31m+     demo: boolean("demo").default(false).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_user_id ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     campaignIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_campaign_id ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (campaign_id)`,[39m
[31m+     statusIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_status ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (status)`,[39m
[31m+     userCampaignIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_user_campaign ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id, campaign_id)`,[39m
[31m+     userStatusIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_user_status ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id, status)`,[39m
[31m+     callbackIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_callback_scheduled_at ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id, callback_scheduled_at) WHERE callback_scheduled_at IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     // Global due-scan for the callback tick (no user_id predicate) — the[39m
[31m+     // user-leading partial index above cannot serve it.[39m
[31m+     callbackDueGlobalIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_callback_due_global ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (callback_scheduled_at) WHERE callback_scheduled_at IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     createdAtIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_created_at ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (created_at)`,[39m
[31m+     userCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_user_created ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     statusNextAttemptIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_status_next_attempt ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (status, callback_scheduled_at) WHERE status IN ('Pending', 'Retrying')`,[39m
[31m+     userCustomerNumberIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_leads_user_customer_number ON wireline_outbound_leads USING btree (user_id, customer_number)`,[39m
[31m+     userIdempotencyUniqueIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbound_leads_user_idempotency ON wireline_outbound_leads (user_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     userPhoneTailIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbound_leads_user_phone_tail ON wireline_outbound_leads (user_id, right(regexp_replace(phone_number, '[^0-9]', '', 'g'), 10))`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.5 REAL-TIME SIGNAL PRESENCE[39m
[31m+ export const signalPresence = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_signal_presence",[39m
[31m+   {[39m

[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
