[31m+ // 3.7 MOBILE DEVICES (Wireline — APNs / PushKit VoIP push targets)[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // One row per registered iOS device per user. The native Wireline app[39m
[31m+ // registers its PushKit VoIP token here after sign-in so the backend can[39m
[31m+ // wake the device with a CallKit-reportable VoIP push when an inbound call[39m
[31m+ // arrives (see src/lib/apns-push.ts). The separate alert token is used for[39m
[31m+ // non-call notifications (e.g. mid-call tool dispatch). FK cascade removes[39m
[31m+ // rows when the user is deleted; the (user_id, device_id) unique key makes[39m
[31m+ // token refresh an idempotent upsert.[39m
[31m+ export const mobileDevices = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_mobile_devices",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+     deviceId: text("device_id").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     apnsVoipToken: text("apns_voip_token"),[39m
[31m+     apnsAlertToken: text("apns_alert_token"),[39m
[31m+     // Android FCM registration token. One token per device handles both the[39m
[31m+     // wake (high-priority data message → ConnectionService incoming call) and[39m
[31m+     // alert (recap / device-action) shapes, unlike APNs' split voip/alert pair.[39m
[31m+     fcmToken: text("fcm_token"),[39m
[31m+     platform: text("platform").default("ios").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     appVersion: text("app_version"),[39m
[31m+     lastSeenAt: timestamp("last_seen_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+     updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   (t: any) => ({[39m
[31m+     userDeviceUnique: unique("wireline_mobile_devices_user_device_key").on(t.userId, t.deviceId),[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mobile_devices_user_id ON wireline_mobile_devices USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 3.8 PREPAID CREDIT WALLET (Wireline)[39m
[31m+ //[39m
[31m+ // Append-only ledger of USD credit movements. Positive deltas are top-ups[39m
[31m+ // (purchased on the WEB via Stripe — never in-app, to stay clear of Apple's[39m
[31m+ // IAP rules; the iOS app is read-only on billing). Negative deltas are debits[39m
[31m+ // that absorb voice-minute overage before Stripe metering kicks in. Balance is[39m
[31m+ // the sum of deltas; balance_after is denormalized per row for audit + fast[39m
[31m+ // reads. Existing tenants simply have no rows (balance 0) and bill exactly as[39m
[31m+ // before.[39m
[31m+ export const creditLedger = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_credit_ledger",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),[39m
[31m+     deltaUsd: decimal("delta_usd", { precision: 12, scale: 4 }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     balanceAfterUsd: decimal("balance_after_usd", {[39m
[31m+       precision: 12,[39m
[31m+       scale: 4,[39m
[31m+     }).notNull(),[39m
[31m+     entryKind: text("entry_kind").notNull().default("adjustment"),[39m
[31m+     reason: text("reason").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     customerReason: text("customer_reason"),[39m
[31m+     refType: text("ref_type"),[39m
[31m+     refId: text("ref_id"),[39m
[31m+     idempotencyKey: text("idempotency_key"),[39m
[31m+     source: text("source").notNull().default("wireline"),[39m
[31m+     provider: text("provider"),[39m
[31m+     providerEventId: text("provider_event_id"),[39m
[31m+     providerObjectId: text("provider_object_id"),[39m
[31m+     metadata: jsonb("metadata")[39m
[31m+       .notNull()[39m
[31m+       .default(sql`'{}'::jsonb`),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   (t: any) => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_credit_ledger_user ON wireline_credit_ledger (user_id, created_at DESC)`,[39m
[31m+     idempotencyIdx: sql`CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS wireline_credit_ledger_idempotency_key ON wireline_credit_ledger (user_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     providerIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_credit_ledger_provider ON wireline_credit_ledger (provider, provider_event_id, provider_object_id) WHERE provider IS NOT NULL`,[39m
[31m+     // Idempotent top-ups/debits: a given (user, ref_type, ref_id) lands once.[39m
[31m+     refUnique: unique("wireline_credit_ledger_ref_key").on(t.userId, t.refType, t.refId),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // 4. AUDIT LOG (AI SPEND & COMPLIANCE)[39m
[31m+ export const auditLog = pgTable([39m
[31m+   "wireline_audit_log",[39m
[31m+   {[39m
[31m+     id: bigint("id", { mode: "bigint" }).primaryKey().generatedByDefaultAsIdentity(),[39m
[31m+     // Nullable in the DB (see migration 0075): system security events —[39m
[31m+     // failed logins, unauthenticated rate-limit hits, cron auth rejections —[39m
[31m+     // legitimately carry no user id. `actor_email` captures the pre-auth[39m
[31m+     // identity when we have one.[39m
[31m+     userId: uuid("user_id"),[39m
[31m+     action: text("action").notNull(),[39m
[31m+     provider: text("provider"),[39m
[31m+     model: text("model"),[39m
[31m+     inputTokens: integer("input_tokens").default(0),[39m
[31m+     outputTokens: integer("output_tokens").default(0),[39m
[31m+     cacheReadTokens: integer("cache_read_tokens").default(0),[39m
[31m+     cacheCreateTokens: integer("cache_create_tokens").default(0),[39m
[31m+     costUsd: decimal("cost_usd", { precision: 12, scale: 6 }).default("0"),[39m
[31m+     latencyMs: integer("latency_ms"),[39m
[31m+     metadata: jsonb("metadata"),[39m
[31m+     eventType: text("event_type").notNull().default("ai.api_call"),[39m
[31m+     severity: text("severity").notNull().default("info"),[39m
[31m+     actorEmail: text("actor_email"),[39m
[31m+     resourceType: text("resource_type"),[39m
[31m+     resourceId: text("resource_id"),[39m
[31m+     ipAddress: text("ip_address"),[39m
[31m+     userAgent: text("user_agent"),[39m
[31m+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),[39m
[31m+   },[39m
[31m+   () => ({[39m
[31m+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_user_id ON wireline_audit_log USING btree (user_id)`,[39m
[31m+     eventTypeIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_event_type ON wireline_audit_log USING btree (event_type)`,[39m
[31m+     userCreatedIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_user_created ON wireline_audit_log USING btree (user_id, created_at)`,[39m
[31m+     createdAtIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_created_at ON wireline_audit_log USING btree (created_at)`,[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ );[39m

[31m+ // 4.4 BILLING CACHE[39m
[31m+ export const subscriptions = pgTable("wireline_subscriptions", {[39m

[41m[1m FAIL [22m[49m tests/subsystem-audit-hardening-contract.test.ts[2m > [22mmarketing email honors suppression and unsubscribe on every transport[2m > [22mroutes cadence email steps as marketing mail
[31m[1mAssertionError[22m: expected '/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/…' to match /integ\.sendEmail\(\{[\s\S]{0,200}mark…/[39m

[32m- Expected:[39m
/integ\.sendEmail\(\{[\s\S]{0,200}marketing: true/

[31m+ Received:[39m
"/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any -- module operates on heterogeneous data shapes across integrations. */
/**
 * Cadence engine — fires scheduled call, SMS, and email steps. Every call step
 * routes through Wireline's native Twilio + xAI placement service and carries
 * the existing lead id so carrier, realtime, transcript, and settlement state
 * remain attached to one authoritative record.
 */
import { and, asc, eq, inArray, lte, sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";

import * as schema from \"../db/schema\";
import { IntegrationService } from \"./integrations\";
import { evaluateOutboundDialGate, mergeLeadMetadata } from \"./outbound-dial-gate\";
import { OUTBOUND_CONSENT_VERSION } from \"./outbound-call-purpose\";
import { drizzle, log } from \"./server-context\";
import { jsonSafeId, toJsonbSafe } from \"./server-utils\";
import { runTenantProviderWorkWithDeletionFence } from \"./tenant-provider-deletion-fence\";
import { sendTenantMessage } from \"./twilio-service\";
import { placeOutboundCall } from \"./voice/outbound-call-service\";
import { assertOutboundVoiceAllowed } from \"./voice-spend-control\";

interface CadenceStep {
  type: \"call\" | \"sms\" | \"email\";
  delayHours: number;
  template?: string;
  agentId?: string;
}

interface CadenceRow {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  name: string;
  agentId: string | null;
  steps: CadenceStep[];
}

interface LeadRow {
  id: bigint;
  customerName: string | null;
  customerNumber: string | null;
  email: string | null;
  agentId: string | null;
  lastCalledAt: Date | null;
  metadata: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}

function applyVars(template: string, lead: LeadRow): string {
  return template
    .replace(/\\{\\{\\s*name\\s*\\}\\}/gi, lead.customerName?.split(\" \")[0] || \"there\")
    .replace(/\\{\\{\\s*full_name\\s*\\}\\}/gi, lead.customerName || \"\")
    .replace(/\\{\\{\\s*phone\\s*\\}\\}/gi, lead.customerNumber || \"\")
    .replace(/\\{\\{\\s*email\\s*\\}\\}/gi, lead.email || \"\");
}

async function stampCadenceComplianceDenial(
  cadence: CadenceRow,
  lead: LeadRow,
  gate: { status: string; reason: string; metadataPatch?: Record<string, unknown>; consentLogEvent?: string },
): Promise<void> {
  await drizzle
    .update(schema.outboundLeads)
    .set({
      status: gate.status,
      lastContactSummary: `Cadence blocked: ${gate.reason}.`,
      metadata: toJsonbSafe(mergeLeadMetadata(lead.metadata || {}, gate.metadataPatch)) as any,
    })
    .where(and(eq(schema.outboundLeads.id, lead.id), eq(schema.outboundLeads.userId, cadence.userId)));
  if (gate.consentLogEvent && lead.customerNumber) {
    await drizzle.insert(schema.consentLog).values({
      userId: cadence.userId,
      customerNumber: lead.customerNumber,
      event: gate.consentLogEvent,
      state: \"blocked\",
      metadata: toJsonbSafe({
        leadId: jsonSafeId(lead.id),
        cadenceId: cadence.id,
        source: \"cadence\",
        ...(gate.metadataPatch?.tcpa ? { tcpa: gate.metadataPatch.tcpa } : {}),
      }),
    });
  }
}

/**
 * `retryable` is reserved for failures proven to occur before a provider-side
 * effect. Native call creation treats transport/5xx ambiguity as do-not-retry,
 * so a cadence never places a second marketing call merely because the first
 * carrier response was lost.
 */
async function executeStep(
  step: CadenceStep,
  cadence: CadenceRow,
  lead: LeadRow,
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; result: string; defer?: boolean; retryable?: boolean; failRun?: boolean }> {
  const agentId = step.agentId || cadence.agentId || lead.agentId;

  if ((step.type === \"call\" || step.type === \"sms\") && lead.customerNumber) {
    try {
      const gate = await evaluateOutboundDialGate({
        userId: cadence.userId,
        phone: lead.customerNumber,
        leadId: lead.id,
        lastCalledAt: lead.lastCalledAt,
        metadata: lead.metadata || {},
      });
      if (!gate.allowed) {
        const temporal =
          gate.reason === \"outside_permitted_calling_hours\" ||
          gate.reason === \"self_recent_call_scheduled\";
        if (!temporal && gate.status !== \"AutoDialFailed\") {
          await stampCadenceComplianceDenial(cadence, lead, gate);
          return {
            ok: false,
            result: `skipped: ${gate.reason}`,
            failRun: true,
          };
        }
        if (gate.reason === \"federal_dnc_unconfigured\") {
          return { ok: false, result: `skipped: ${gate.reason}`, retryable: true };
        }
        return { ok: false, result: `skipped: ${gate.reason}`, defer: temporal };
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      log.warn(\"cadence outbound compliance gate failed, failing closed\", {
        error: err?.message,
      });
      return { ok: false, result: \"compliance gate failed (fail-closed)\", retryable: true };
    }
  }

  if (step.type === \"call\") {
    if (!lead.customerNumber) return { ok: false, result: \"no phone number on lead\" };
    if (!agentId) return { ok: false, result: \"no agent configured for call step\" };
    try {
      await assertOutboundVoiceAllowed({ userId: cadence.userId, source: \"cadence\", units: 0 });
    } catch (error: any) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        result: `voice control rejected: ${error?.message || \"blocked\"}`,
        retryable: true,
      };
    }

    const result = await placeOutboundCall({
      userId: cadence.userId,
      agentId,
      customerName: lead.customerName,
      customerNumber: lead.customerNumber,
      context: `Cadence \"${cadence.name}\", step type call`,
      callPurpose: \"marketing_or_sales\",
      leadId: lead.id,
    });
    if (result.ok) {
      try {
        await Promise.all([
          drizzle
            .update(schema.outboundLeads)
            .set({
              lastCalledAt: new Date(),
              attempts: sql`${schema.outboundLeads.attempts} + 1`,
              callId: result.callId,
            })
            .where(eq(schema.outboundLeads.id, lead.id)),
          drizzle.insert(schema.consentLog).values({
            userId: cadence.userId,
            customerNumber: lead.customerNumber,
            callId: result.callId,
            event: \"outbound_call_placed\",
            state: \"initiated\",
            metadata: toJsonbSafe({
              leadId: jsonSafeId(lead.id),
              cadenceId: cadence.id,
              call_purpose: \"marketing_or_sales\",
              consent_version: OUTBOUND_CONSENT_VERSION,
              source: \"cadence\",
            }),
          }),
        ]);
      } catch (recordError: any) {
        log.error(\"cadence call placed but dial evidence write failed\", {
          leadId: jsonSafeId(lead.id),
          cadenceId: cadence.id,
          error: recordError?.message,
        });
      }
    }
    return {
      ok: result.ok,
      result: result.ok
        ? `call placed ${result.callId}`
        : result.providerOutcomeAmbiguous
          ? `call outcome uncertain; automatic retry suppressed: ${result.error}`
          : `call failed: ${result.error}`,
      // Native placement marks ambiguous outcomes do-not-retry. Advancing the
      // cadence is safer than risking a duplicate call to the same consumer.
    };
  }

  if (step.type === \"sms\") {
    if (!lead.customerNumber) return { ok: false, result: \"no phone number on lead\" };
    const body = applyVars(step.template || `Hi {{name}}, checking back in. Wireline`, lead);
    const result = await sendTenantMessage(cadence.userId, {
      to: lead.customerNumber,
      body,
      agentId: cadence.agentId ?? lead.agentId ?? undefined,
    });
    return {
      ok: result.ok,
      result: result.ok
        ? `sms sent ${result.sid || \"\"}`
        : `sms failed: ${result.reason || \"unknown\"}`,
    };
  }

  if (step.type === \"email\") {
    if (!lead.email) return { ok: false, result: \"no email on lead\" };
    const body = applyVars(step.template || `Hi {{name}},\\n\\nFollowing up. Wireline`, lead);
    const integration = new IntegrationService(cadence.userId);
    const ok = await integration.sendEmail({
      to: lead.email,
      subject: `Following up: ${cadence.name}`,
      body,
      marketing: true,
    });
    return { ok, result: ok ? \"email sent\" : \"email failed (suppressed or no connected mailbox?)\" };
  }

  return { ok: false, result: `unknown step type ${step.type}` };
}

const CADENCE_STEP_LEASE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
const CADENCE_TEMPORAL_DEFER_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
const CADENCE_EXCEPTION_RETRY_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
const STEP_EXCEPTION_RETRY_PREFIX = \"step exception (will retry): \";

export async function tickDueCadenceRuns({ limit = 50 }: { limit?: number }): Promise<{
  processed: number;
  advanced: number;
  completed: number;
  failed: number;
  deferred: number;
}> {
  const now = new Date();
  const dueRuns = await drizzle
    .select()
    .from(schema.cadenceRuns)
    .where(and(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.status, \"active\"), lte(schema.cadenceRuns.nextDueAt, now)))
    .orderBy(asc(schema.cadenceRuns.nextDueAt))
    .limit(limit);

  let advanced = 0;
  let completed = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  let deferred = 0;

  const cadenceIds = [...new Set(dueRuns.map((run) => run.cadenceId).filter(Boolean))] as string[];
  const leadIds = [
    ...new Set(dueRuns.map((run) => run.leadId).filter((value): value is bigint => value != null)),
  ];

  const [cadenceRows, leadRows] = await Promise.all([
    cadenceIds.length
      ? drizzle.select().from(schema.cadences).where(inArray(schema.cadences.id, cadenceIds))
      : Promise.resolve([]),
    leadIds.length
      ? drizzle
          .select({
            id: schema.outboundLeads.id,
            userId: schema.outboundLeads.userId,
            customerName: schema.outboundLeads.customerName,
            customerNumber: schema.outboundLeads.customerNumber,
            email: schema.outboundLeads.email,
            agentId: schema.outboundLeads.agentId,
            lastCalledAt: schema.outboundLeads.lastCalledAt,
            metadata: schema.outboundLeads.metadata,
          })
          .from(schema.outboundLeads)
          .where(inArray(schema.outboundLeads.id, leadIds))
      : Promise.resolve([]),
  ]);

  const cadenceById = new Map(cadenceRows.map((cadence) => [cadence.id, cadence]));
  const leadById = new Map(leadRows.map((lead) => [lead.id, lead]));

  for (const run of dueRuns) {
    try {
      const claimed = await drizzle
        .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
        .set({ nextDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + CADENCE_STEP_LEASE_MS), updatedAt: now })
        .where(
          and(
            eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id),
            eq(schema.cadenceRuns.status, \"active\"),
            lte(schema.cadenceRuns.nextDueAt, now),
          ),
        )
        .returning({ id: schema.cadenceRuns.id });
      if (!claimed.length) continue;

      const cadenceRowRaw = cadenceById.get(run.cadenceId);
      const cadenceRow =
        cadenceRowRaw && cadenceRowRaw.userId === run.userId ? cadenceRowRaw : undefined;
      if (!cadenceRow || cadenceRow.active === false) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({ status: \"paused\", updatedAt: now, lastResult: \"cadence inactive or deleted\" })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        continue;
      }

      const leadRowRaw = leadById.get(run.leadId);
      const leadRow = leadRowRaw && leadRowRaw.userId === run.userId ? leadRowRaw : undefined;
      if (!leadRow) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({ status: \"failed\", updatedAt: now, lastResult: \"lead no longer exists\" })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        failed++;
        continue;
      }

      const steps = (cadenceRow.steps as CadenceStep[]) || [];
      const currentIndex = run.currentStep ?? 0;
      const step = steps[currentIndex];
      if (!step) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({ status: \"completed\", updatedAt: now, lastResult: \"all steps complete\" })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        completed++;
        continue;
      }

      const fencedStep = await runTenantProviderWorkWithDeletionFence({
        userId: run.userId,
        perform: () => executeStep(step, cadenceRow as any, leadRow as any),
      });
      if (!fencedStep.performed) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({
            status: \"paused\",
            nextDueAt: null,
            lastResult: \"account deletion in progress, provider step cancelled\",
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        continue;
      }
      const stepResult = fencedStep.result;

      if (stepResult.defer) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({
            nextDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + CADENCE_TEMPORAL_DEFER_MS),
            lastResult: stepResult.result,
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        deferred++;
        continue;
      }

      if (stepResult.failRun) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({
            status: \"paused\",
            nextDueAt: null,
            lastResult: stepResult.result,
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        continue;
      }

      if (!stepResult.ok && stepResult.retryable) {
        const alreadyRetried = (run.lastResult || \"\").startsWith(STEP_EXCEPTION_RETRY_PREFIX);
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set(
            alreadyRetried
              ? { status: \"failed\", lastResult: stepResult.result, updatedAt: now }
              : {
                  nextDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + CADENCE_EXCEPTION_RETRY_MS),
                  lastResult: `${STEP_EXCEPTION_RETRY_PREFIX}${stepResult.result}`,
                  updatedAt: now,
                },
          )
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        if (alreadyRetried) failed++;
        else deferred++;
        continue;
      }

      const nextIndex = currentIndex + 1;
      const nextStep = steps[nextIndex];
      if (!nextStep) {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({
            currentStep: nextIndex,
            nextDueAt: null,
            status: \"completed\",
            lastResult: stepResult.result,
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        completed++;
      } else {
        await drizzle
          .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
          .set({
            currentStep: nextIndex,
            nextDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + nextStep.delayHours * 3_600_000),
            lastResult: stepResult.result,
            updatedAt: now,
          })
          .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
        advanced++;
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      log.error(\"cadence tick run failed\", { runId: run.id, error: error?.message });
      const alreadyRetried = (run.lastResult || \"\").startsWith(STEP_EXCEPTION_RETRY_PREFIX);
      await drizzle
        .update(schema.cadenceRuns)
        .set(
          alreadyRetried
            ? { status: \"failed\", lastResult: error?.message || \"step exception\", updatedAt: now }
            : {
                nextDueAt: new Date(Date.now() + CADENCE_EXCEPTION_RETRY_MS),
                lastResult: `${STEP_EXCEPTION_RETRY_PREFIX}${error?.message || \"step exception\"}`,
                updatedAt: now,
              },
        )
        .where(eq(schema.cadenceRuns.id, run.id));
      if (alreadyRetried) failed++;
    }
  }

  return { processed: dueRuns.length, advanced, completed, failed, deferred };
}
"

[31m+ function twilioStatus(error: unknown): number | null {[39m
[31m+   const status = Number((error as { status?: unknown } | null)?.status);[39m
[31m+   return Number.isInteger(status) && status >= 100 && status <= 599 ? status : null;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function claimDeactivation(input: {[39m
[31m+   userId: string;[39m
[31m+   phoneNumber: string;[39m
[31m+ }): Promise<DeactivationClaim | null> {[39m
[31m+   const token = randomUUID();[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     return await drizzle.transaction(async (tx) => {[39m
[31m+       const result = (await tx.execute(sql`[39m
[31m+         SELECT[39m
[31m+           binding_id,[39m
[31m+           claim_token,[39m
[31m+           twilio_phone_number_sid,[39m
[31m+           twilio_sip_trunk_sid,[39m
[31m+           prior_last_error[39m
[31m+         FROM public.wireline_claim_xai_phone_deactivation([39m
[31m+           ${input.userId}::uuid,[39m
[31m+           ${input.phoneNumber},[39m
[31m+           ${token}::uuid[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+       `)) as unknown as { rows?: DeactivationClaimRow[] };[39m
[31m+       const row = result.rows?.[0];[39m
[31m+       if (!row) return null;[39m
[31m+       if (!row.binding_id || row.claim_token !== token) {[39m
[31m+         throw AppError.conflict("The native phone deactivation lease was replaced.");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       return {[39m
[31m+         bindingId: row.binding_id,[39m
[31m+         token,[39m
[31m+         twilioPhoneNumberSid: row.twilio_phone_number_sid,[39m
[31m+         twilioSipTrunkSid: row.twilio_sip_trunk_sid,[39m
[31m+         priorLastError: row.prior_last_error,[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     if (error instanceof AppError) throw error;[39m
[31m+     const message = getErrorMessage(error);[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_ALREADY_PROVISIONING")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.conflict([39m
[31m+         "This phone line is currently changing routing. Retry after that operation finishes.",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_OWNED_BY_ANOTHER_TENANT")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.conflict("This phone registration is active in another workspace.");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_OWNERSHIP_MISMATCH")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.conflict("This phone line is no longer owned by this workspace.");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_TENANT_MISMATCH")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.fromHttpStatus(403, "The phone operation is not bound to this workspace.");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_INVALID_CLAIM")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.badRequest("The native phone deactivation claim is invalid.");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     throw error;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function settleDeactivation([39m
[31m+   userId: string,[39m
[31m+   claim: DeactivationClaim,[39m
[31m+   patch: Partial<typeof xaiPhoneBindings.$inferInsert>,[39m
[31m+ ) {[39m
[31m+   const [updated] = await drizzle[39m
[31m+     .update(xaiPhoneBindings)[39m
[31m+     .set({ ...patch, updatedAt: new Date() })[39m
[31m+     .where([39m
[31m+       and([39m
[31m+         eq(xaiPhoneBindings.id, claim.bindingId),[39m
[31m+         eq(xaiPhoneBindings.userId, userId),[39m
[31m+         eq(xaiPhoneBindings.provisioningToken, claim.token),[39m
[31m+       ),[39m
[31m+     )[39m
[31m+     .returning({ id: xaiPhoneBindings.id });[39m
[31m+   if (!updated) {[39m
[31m+     throw AppError.conflict("The native phone deactivation lease expired or was replaced.");[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Detach a line from the native SIP trunk before it becomes unassigned. xAI's[39m
[31m+  * public Direct SIP API documents registration but no deletion endpoint, so the[39m
[31m+  * durable registration is retained as `retired` and can be reactivated without[39m
[31m+  * inventing an unsupported provider operation or losing its one-time secret.[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * The database claim uses the same E.164 advisory lock as activation. Release[39m
[31m+  * and reassignment therefore cannot cross an in-flight Twilio/xAI operation,[39m
[31m+  * and an ambiguous detach remains blocked from automatic reuse until a later[39m
[31m+  * deactivation pass or an operator reconciles provider state.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export async function deactivateNativeXAIPhoneLine(input: {[39m
[31m+   userId: string;[39m
[31m+   phoneNumber: string;[39m
[31m+ }): Promise<void> {[39m
[31m+   const phoneNumber = normalizeXAIPhoneNumber(input.phoneNumber);[39m
[31m+   if (!phoneNumber) throw AppError.badRequest("A valid phone line is required.");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const claim = await claimDeactivation({ userId: input.userId, phoneNumber });[39m
[31m+   if (!claim) return;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   let providerMutationAttempted = false;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     if (claim.twilioSipTrunkSid && claim.twilioPhoneNumberSid) {[39m
[31m+       const client = getTwilioClient();[39m
[31m+       if (!client) throw AppError.serviceUnavailable("Twilio voice is not configured.");[39m
[31m+       const trunk = client.trunking.v1.trunks(claim.twilioSipTrunkSid);[39m
[31m+       const attached = await trunk.phoneNumbers.list({ limit: 1_000 });[39m
[31m+       // Twilio's Elastic SIP Trunk PhoneNumber resource is identified by its[39m
[31m+       // PN `sid`; `phoneNumberSid` is a create parameter, not an instance field.[39m
[31m+       const association = attached.find((row) => row.sid === claim.twilioPhoneNumberSid);[39m
[31m+       const associationSid = String(association?.sid || "").trim();[39m
[31m+       if (association && associationSid) {[39m
[31m+         providerMutationAttempted = true;[39m
[31m+         try {[39m
[31m+           await trunk.phoneNumbers(associationSid).remove();[39m
[31m+         } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+           if (twilioStatus(error) !== 404) throw error;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     await settleDeactivation(input.userId, claim, {[39m
[31m+       status: "retired",[39m
[31m+       provisioningToken: null,[39m
[31m+       provisioningClaimedAt: null,[39m
[31m+       // A registration whose one-time xAI result was ambiguous may be detached[39m
[31m+       // from Twilio, but it must never become automatically reusable.[39m
[31m+       lastError: claim.priorLastError === XAI_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS ? XAI_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS : null,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     log.info("Native Twilio routing detached and registration retired", {[39m
[31m+       userId: input.userId,[39m
[31m+       bindingId: claim.bindingId,[39m
[31m+       phoneSuffix: phoneNumber.slice(-4),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     const ambiguous = providerMutationAttempted && isAmbiguousTwilioPhoneMutationError(error);[39m
[31m+     const failureCode =[39m
[31m+       claim.priorLastError === XAI_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS[39m
[31m+         ? XAI_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS[39m
[31m+         : ambiguous[39m
[31m+           ? XAI_DEACTIVATION_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS[39m
[31m+           : XAI_DEACTIVATION_FAILED;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await settleDeactivation(input.userId, claim, {[39m
[31m+         status: "error",[39m
[31m+         provisioningToken: null,[39m
[31m+         provisioningClaimedAt: null,[39m
[31m+         lastError: failureCode,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } catch (persistError) {[39m
[31m+       log.error("Native phone deactivation failure state could not be persisted", {[39m
[31m+         userId: input.userId,[39m
[31m+         bindingId: claim.bindingId,[39m
[31m+         priorState: claim.priorLastError,[39m
[31m+         error: getErrorMessage(persistError),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     log.error("Native Twilio routing could not be safely detached", {[39m
[31m+       userId: input.userId,[39m
[31m+       bindingId: claim.bindingId,[39m
[31m+       phoneSuffix: phoneNumber.slice(-4),[39m
[31m+       ambiguous,[39m
[31m+       error: getErrorMessage(error),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     if (error instanceof AppError) throw error;[39m
[31m+     throw AppError.serviceUnavailable([39m
[31m+       ambiguous[39m
[31m+         ? "The carrier detach outcome is uncertain. Automatic reuse is blocked until provider state is reconciled."[39m
[31m+         : "The phone line could not be detached from native voice. No local ownership change was committed.",[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * The low-level provisioner owns the complete lease: Twilio trunk verification,[39m
[31m+  * phone attachment, xAI registration, and final activation all finish before[39m
[31m+  * status returns to active. No provider-side repair runs after the lease closes.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export async function provisionNativeXAIPhone(input: {[39m
[31m+   userId: string;[39m
[31m+   agentId: string;[39m
[31m+ }): Promise<NativeXAIPhoneStatus> {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     return await provisionNativeXAIPhoneLowLevel(input);[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     const message = getErrorMessage(error);[39m
[31m+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_DEACTIVATION_UNRESOLVED")) {[39m
[31m+       throw AppError.serviceUnavailable([39m
[31m+         "Phone activation is blocked until the previous carrier detach is reconciled.",[39m
[31m+       );[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     throw error;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m

IDENTITY & ACCOUNTS%0A+%0A+ /**%0A+  * A user can own one or two accounts — a personal account (single agent,%0A+  * streamlined UI) and/or a business account (AI employees, Studio command%0A+  * surfaces, outbound pipeline). NULL means "unverified" — the trial gate refuses spend%0A+    * actions (outbound dial, new agent creation, Stripe checkout)%0A+    * until this is set. Existing users from before the verification%0A+    * gate shipped are backfilled to their createdAt (auto-verified)%0A+    * by the migration so they don't suddenly hit a paywall.%0A+    */%0A+   emailVerifiedAt: timestamp("email_verified_at", { withTimezone: true }),%0A+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),%0A+   updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),%0A+ });%0A+%0A+ // Single-use password-reset tokens with 1-hour TTL. The token itself is%0A+ // only stored hashed (sha256) so a DB leak doesn't enable account takeover.%0A+ export const passwordResetTokens = pgTable("wireline_password_reset_tokens", {%0A+   id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),%0A+   userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),%0A+   tokenHash: text("token_hash").notNull().unique(),%0A+   expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),%0A+   usedAt: timestamp("used_at", { withTimezone: true }),%0A+   requestedIp: text("requested_ip"),%0A+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),%0A+ });%0A+%0A+ /**%0A+  * Single-use email-verification tokens with 24-hour TTL. Owned by a user; the%0A+  * email-service generates the plaintext token, stores only the sha256,%0A+  * and emails the plaintext to the user. They click the link, we look%0A+  * up by hash, verify expiry + usedAt, then set users.emailVerifiedAt%0A+  * and mark the token as used.%0A+  *%0A+  * Resend is rate-limited (1 token per minute per user) at the route%0A+  * layer to prevent abuse.%0A+  */%0A+ export const emailVerificationTokens = pgTable("wireline_email_verification_tokens", {%0A+   id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),%0A+   userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),%0A+   tokenHash: text("token_hash").notNull().unique(),%0A+   expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),%0A+   usedAt: timestamp("used_at", { withTimezone: true }),%0A+   createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),%0A+ });%0A+%0A+ // 1. SETTINGS & BILLING%0A+ export const settings = pgTable("wireline_settings", {%0A+   id: text("id").primaryKey(),%0A+   // FK with cascade so deleting a user removes their settings row in%0A+   // the same transaction — no orphans, no manual cleanup. Assistants INVENTORY (AGENTS)%0A+ export const agents = pgTable(%0A+   "wireline_agents",%0A+   {%0A+     id: text("id").primaryKey(),%0A+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),%0A+     /** Scopes the agent to a specific account. When set, the Vapi%0A+      * webhook listens for `language-change-detected` events and hands%0A+      * the live call off to a clone of this assistant with the secondary%0A+      * voiceId active — so the caller hears a native-sounding speaker%0A+      * the instant the transcriber flips from `en` to a non-English%0A+      * locale (typically Spanish for our customers).%0A+      *%0A+      * Optional. Account Holders define one once; the%0A+  * sentinel job worker ticks through it for every lead enrolled.%0A+  *%0A+  * Example steps payload:%0A+  *   [%0A+  *     { type: "call",  delayHours: 0  },%0A+  *     { type: "sms",   delayHours: 24, template: "Hi {{name}}, following up on our call..." },%0A+  *     { type: "email", delayHours: 72, template: "..." },%0A+  *     { type: "call",  delayHours: 168 }%0A+  *   ]%0A+  */%0A+ export const cadences = pgTable(%0A+   "wireline_cadences",%0A+   {%0A+     id: uuid("id").primaryKey().defaultRandom(),%0A+     userId: uuid("user_id").notNull(),%0A+     name: text("name").notNull(),%0A+     description: text("description"),%0A+     agentId: text("agent_id"),%0A+     steps: jsonb("steps").notNull(),%0A+     active: boolean("active").default(true),%0A+     createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow(),%0A+   },%0A+   () => ({%0A+     userIdIdx: sql`CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_cadences_user_id ON wireline_cadences (user_id)`,%0A+   }),%0A+ );%0A+%0A+ /**%0A+  * Per-lead cadence execution state. XAI_DEACTIVATION_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS%0A+           : XAI_DEACTIVATION_FAILED;%0A+%0A+     try {%0A+       await settleDeactivation(input.userId, claim, {%0A+         status: "error",%0A+         provisioningToken: null,%0A+         provisioningClaimedAt: null,%0A+         lastError: failureCode,%0A+       });%0A+     } catch (persistError) {%0A+       log.error("Native phone deactivation failure state could not be persisted", {%0A+         userId: input.userId,%0A+         bindingId: claim.bindingId,%0A+         priorState: claim.priorLastError,%0A+         error: getErrorMessage(persistError),%0A+       });%0A+     }%0A+%0A+     log.error("Native Twilio routing could not be safely detached", {%0A+       userId: input.userId,%0A+       bindingId: claim.bindingId,%0A+       phoneSuffix: phoneNumber.slice(-4),%0A+       ambiguous,%0A+       error: getErrorMessage(error),%0A+     });%0A+     if (error instanceof AppError) throw error;%0A+     throw AppError.serviceUnavailable(%0A+       ambiguous%0A+         ? "The carrier detach outcome is uncertain. Automatic reuse is blocked until provider state is reconciled."%0A+         : "The phone line could not be detached from native voice. No local ownership change was committed.",%0A+     );%0A+   }%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ /**%0A+  * The low-level provisioner owns the complete lease: Twilio trunk verification,%0A+  * phone attachment, xAI registration, and final activation all finish before%0A+  * status returns to active. No provider-side repair runs after the lease closes.%0A+  */%0A+ export async function provisionNativeXAIPhone(input: {%0A+   userId: string;%0A+   agentId: string;%0A+ }): Promise<NativeXAIPhoneStatus> {%0A+   try {%0A+     return await provisionNativeXAIPhoneLowLevel(input);%0A+   } catch (error) {%0A+     const message = getErrorMessage(error);%0A+     if (message.includes("XAI_PHONE_DEACTIVATION_UNRESOLVED")) {%0A+       throw AppError.serviceUnavailable(%0A+         "Phone activation is blocked until the previous carrier detach is reconciled.",%0A+       );%0A+     }%0A+     throw error;%0A+   }%0A+ }%0A+%0A%0A ❯ tests/xai-native-voice-contract.test.ts:250:24%0A%0A
